### [1] [Complementarity-Preserving Generative Theory for Multimodal ECG Synthesis: A Quantum-Inspired Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26695)
*Timothy Oladunni,Farouk Ganiyu-Adewumi,Clyde Baidoo,Kyndal Maclin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出CPGT理论，认为多模态ECG生成需保持跨域互补性，而非独立合成各模态。实现Q-CFD-GAN量子启发生成框架，在复数隐空间建模并施加互补性约束，显著提升生成信号的生理一致性和下游应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG多模态生成模型通常独立合成时域、频域和时频域表示，导致生成的ECG数据在视觉上合理但跨域生理不一致。需要一种能保持跨域互补性的生成理论来产生生理有效的多模态信号。

Method: 建立Complementarity-Preserving Generative Theory (CPGT)理论，并通过Q-CFD-GAN框架实现：在复数隐空间中建模多模态ECG结构，施加互补性感知约束来调节互信息、冗余性和形态一致性。

Result: Q-CFD-GAN将隐空间嵌入方差降低82%，分类器可信度误差降低26.6%，三域互补性从0.56恢复到0.91，形态偏差最低（3.8%）。证明保持多模态信息几何结构对生成生理有意义的ECG信号至关重要。

Conclusion: 保持多模态信息几何结构，而非仅优化单模态保真度，对于生成生理有意义且适合下游临床机器学习应用的合成ECG信号至关重要。CPGT理论和Q-CFD-GAN框架为此提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal deep learning has substantially improved electrocardiogram (ECG) classification by jointly leveraging time, frequency, and time-frequency representations. However, existing generative models typically synthesize these modalities independently, resulting in synthetic ECG data that are visually plausible yet physiologically inconsistent across domains. This work establishes a Complementarity-Preserving Generative Theory (CPGT), which posits that physiologically valid multimodal signal generation requires explicit preservation of cross-domain complementarity rather than loosely coupled modality synthesis. We instantiate CPGT through Q-CFD-GAN, a quantum-inspired generative framework that models multimodal ECG structure within a complex-valued latent space and enforces complementarity-aware constraints regulating mutual information, redundancy, and morphological coherence. Experimental evaluation demonstrates that Q-CFD-GAN reduces latent embedding variance by 82%, decreases classifier-based plausibility error by 26.6%, and restores tri-domain complementarity from 0.56 to 0.91, while achieving the lowest observed morphology deviation (3.8%). These findings show that preserving multimodal information geometry, rather than optimizing modality-specific fidelity alone, is essential for generating synthetic ECG signals that remain physiologically meaningful and suitable for downstream clinical machine-learning applications.

</details>


### [2] [EMPD: An Event-based Multimodal Physiological Dataset for Remote Pulse Wave Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26699)
*Qian Feng,Pengfei Li,Rongshan Gao,Jiale Xu,Rui Gong,Yidi Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 首个基于事件相机的非接触式生理感知基准数据集EMPD，通过激光辅助系统将皮肤微振动转换为事件相机可检测信号，包含193个记录，用于开发神经形态生理监测算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于帧的远程光电容积描记术(rPPG)存在运动伪影和时间分辨率有限的问题，需要更鲁棒的生理监测方法。

Method: 使用激光辅助采集系统，将径向动脉的细微皮肤振动调制为神经形态传感器可检测的显著信号。硬件平台集成高分辨率事件相机、工业RGB相机和临床级脉搏血氧仪。

Result: 创建了EMPD数据集，包含83名受试者的193个有效记录，心率范围40-110 BPM，涵盖静息和运动后状态，提供微秒级时间精度的多模态同步数据。

Conclusion: EMPD是首个专门为事件相机非接触生理感知设计的基准数据集，为神经形态生理监测领域的算法开发提供了关键资源。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) based on traditional frame-based cameras often struggles with motion artifacts and limited temporal resolution. To address these limitations, we introduce EMPD (Event-based Multimodal Physiological Dataset), the first benchmark dataset specifically designed for non-contact physiological sensing via event cameras. The dataset leverages a laser-assisted acquisition system where a high-coherence laser modulates subtle skin vibrations from the radial artery into significant signals detectable by a neuromorphic sensor. The hardware platform integrates a high-resolution event camera to capture micro-motions and intensity transients, an industrial RGB camera to provide traditional rPPG benchmarks, and a clinical-grade pulse oximeter to record ground truth PPG waveforms. EMPD contains 193 valid records collected from 83 subjects, covering a wide heart rate range (40-110 BPM) under both resting and post-exercise conditions. By providing precisely synchronized multimodal data with microsecond-level temporal precision, EMPD serves as a crucial resource for developing robust algorithms in the field of neuromorphic physiological monitoring. The dataset is publicly available at: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.18765701

</details>


### [3] [Evaluating Smartphone GNSS Accuracy for Geofenced 6 GHz Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26706)
*Joshua Roy Palathinkal,Hardani Ismu Nabil,Muhammad Iqbal Rochman,Hossein Nasiri,Francis A. Gatsi,Monisha Ghosh*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究首次全面评估了GNSS定位在6GHz频段"地理围栏可变功率"设备合规性中的可靠性，发现环境因素是定位误差的主要来源，室内和密集城区定位精度显著下降，且大量设备使用了不符合FCC监管要求的非美国授权GNSS星座。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国6GHz频段新引入的"地理围栏可变功率"设备依赖GNSS定位来遵守地理围栏限制，但GNSS精度在城区、室内等环境中会显著下降。目前缺乏针对GVP合规性的GNSS可靠性实证研究，需要评估GNSS在实际环境中的表现以及是否符合监管要求。

Method: 使用SigCap Android应用程序，在多种真实环境条件下收集和比较GNSS精度数据，包括：城市与郊区景观对比、不同移动状态（静止、步行、驾驶）、室内与室外设置。同时分析了不同GNSS星座对设备定位的贡献。

Result: 研究发现：1) 设备硬件差异会导致GNSS精度变化，但操作环境是误差的主要来源；2) 室内环境和密集城区会持续降低定位精度；3) 靠近建筑物的室外位置也会产生显著误差，即使是低层建筑附近；4) 大量设备使用了非美国授权的GNSS星座进行定位，这不符合FCC监管要求。

Conclusion: GNSS定位在复杂环境中的不可靠性对GVP设备的安全运行构成风险，需要改进定位技术或调整监管框架。当前大量设备使用不符合监管要求的GNSS星座进行定位，这需要监管机构和设备制造商的关注。

Abstract: The recently deployed 6 GHz spectrum in the U.S. utilizes distinct power categories, with the latest proposed "Geofenced Variable Power" (GVP) category permitting indoor and outdoor operations without continuous Automated Frequency Coordination (AFC) by relying instead on local databases of exclusion zones. Consequently, the safe operation of GVP devices depends entirely on reliable GNSS localization to respect these geofences. However, GNSS accuracy is highly variable and significantly degrades in environments like urban canyons or indoors. This paper presents the first comprehensive empirical study evaluating GNSS reliability specifically for GVP compliance. Utilizing the SigCap Android application, we document and compare GNSS accuracy across an extensive array of real-world conditions, encompassing urban versus suburban landscapes, varying mobility states (stationary, walking, driving), and indoor versus outdoor settings. The results demonstrate that while device hardware causes variations in GNSS accuracy, the operational environment is the primary driver of error. Indoor settings and dense urban areas consistently degrade localization. Moreover, outdoor positions adjacent to buildings often surprisingly produce significant inaccuracies, even near low-elevation structures. We further analyze the contribution of different GNSS constellations to device positioning and show that satellites from non-U.S.-licensed constellations-although currently used in a substantial portion of location fixes-are not permitted for regulatory geolocation under FCC requirements.

</details>


### [4] [FEMBA on the Edge: Physiologically-Aware Pre-Training, Quantization, and Deployment of a Bidirectional Mamba EEG Foundation Model on an Ultra-low Power Microcontroller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26716)
*Anna Tegon,Nicholas Lehmann,Yawei Li,Andrea Cossettini,Luca Benini,Thorir Mar Ingolfsson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: FEMBA：首个可部署在超低功耗可穿戴设备上的双向Mamba架构EEG基础模型，通过生理感知预训练和量化感知训练实现高效压缩，在边缘设备上实现实时神经监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Transformer EEG基础模型在可穿戴设备上的计算瓶颈，以及状态空间模型（SSM）的量化挑战，实现长期连续神经监测。

Method: 提出双向Mamba架构FEMBA，在21,000小时EEG数据上预训练；引入生理感知预训练目标（低通滤波重建）；采用量化感知训练压缩至2位权重；在GAP9 RISC-V微控制器上部署，使用双缓冲内存流方案。

Result: 低通预训练将TUAB下游任务AUROC从0.863提升至0.893；QAT成功压缩权重且性能损失可忽略，而标准后训练量化精度下降约30%；嵌入式实现实现确定性实时推理（1.70秒/5秒窗口），内存占用减少74%（约2MB），FLOPs比Transformer基准减少27倍。

Conclusion: FEMBA证明Mamba基础模型可在极端边缘硬件上有效量化和部署，不牺牲临床分析所需的表示质量，为癫痫和睡眠障碍的连续监测建立首个全栈框架。

Abstract: Objective: To enable continuous, long-term neuro-monitoring on wearable devices by overcoming the computational bottlenecks of Transformer-based Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models and the quantization challenges inherent to State-Space Models (SSMs). Methods: We present FEMBA, a bidirectional Mamba architecture pre-trained on over 21,000 hours of EEG. We introduce a novel Physiologically-Aware pre-training objective, consisting of a reconstruction with low-pass filtering, to prioritize neural oscillations over high-frequency artifacts. To address the activation outliers common in SSMs, we employ Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) to compress the model to 2-bit weights. The framework is deployed on a parallel ultra-low-power RISC-V microcontroller (GAP9) using a custom double-buffered memory streaming scheme. Results: The proposed low-pass pre-training improves downstream AUROC on TUAB from 0.863 to 0.893 and AUPR from 0.862 to 0.898 compared to the best contrastive baseline. QAT successfully compresses weights with negligible performance loss, whereas standard post-training quantization degrades accuracy by approximately \textbf{30\%}. The embedded implementation achieves deterministic real-time inference (\textbf{1.70~s} per 5~s window) and reduces the memory footprint by \textbf{74\%} (to $\approx$2~MB), achieving competitive accuracy with up to \textbf{27$\times$} fewer FLOPs than Transformer benchmarks. Conclusion: FEMBA demonstrates that Mamba-based foundation models can be effectively quantized and deployed on extreme-edge hardware without sacrificing the representation quality required for robust clinical analysis. Significance: This work establishes the first full-stack framework for deploying large-scale EEG foundation models on ultra-low-power wearables, facilitating continuous, SSM based monitoring for epilepsy and sleep disorders.

</details>


### [5] [Stress Classification from ECG Signals Using Vision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26721)
*Zeeshan Ahmad,Naimul Khan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Vision Transformer的多级压力评估方法，将ECG信号转换为2D频谱图，通过注意力机制处理被试间变异性，在多个数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers在计算机视觉领域表现出色，但尚未应用于ECG等生理信号的压力评估。传统方法面临被试间变异性的挑战，需要更鲁棒的表示学习方法。

Method: 将原始ECG数据通过短时傅里叶变换转换为2D频谱图，分割成补丁后输入Transformer编码器。同时与1D CNN和ResNet-18进行对比实验，采用留一被试交叉验证。

Result: 在RML数据集上三分类准确率达71.01%，WESAD数据集上三分类76.7%，二分类88.3%。Vision Transformer在处理被试间变异性方面显著优于CNN模型，超越了所有先前SOTA方法。

Conclusion: Vision Transformer通过注意力机制能有效处理ECG信号中的被试间变异性，实现端到端的多级压力评估，无需手工特征提取，学习鲁棒表示，性能优于传统CNN方法。

Abstract: Vision Transformers have shown tremendous success in numerous computer vision applications; however, they have not been exploited for stress assessment using physiological signals such as Electrocardiogram (ECG). In order to get the maximum benefit from the vision transformer for multilevel stress assessment, in this paper, we transform the raw ECG data into 2D spectrograms using short time Fourier transform (STFT). These spectrograms are divided into patches for feeding to the transformer encoder. We also perform experiments with 1D CNN and ResNet-18 (CNN model). We perform leave-onesubject-out cross validation (LOSOCV) experiments on WESAD and Ryerson Multimedia Lab (RML) dataset. One of the biggest challenges of LOSOCV based experiments is to tackle the problem of intersubject variability. In this research, we address the issue of intersubject variability and show our success using 2D spectrograms and the attention mechanism of transformer. Experiments show that vision transformer handles the effect of intersubject variability much better than CNN-based models and beats all previous state-of-the-art methods by a considerable margin. Moreover, our method is end-to-end, does not require handcrafted features, and can learn robust representations. The proposed method achieved 71.01% and 76.7% accuracies with RML dataset and WESAD dataset respectively for three class classification and 88.3% for binary classification on WESAD.

</details>


### [6] [Identity leakage through accent cues in voice anonymisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26843)
*Rayane Bakari,Olivier Le Blouch,Nicolas Gengembre,Nicholas Evans,Michele Panariello*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 语音匿名化旨在隐藏说话人身份但保留语言内容，但匿名化后残留的口音等非音色线索仍可能暴露身份和敏感社会人口特征


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音匿名化系统主要关注音色特征，但忽略了口音等非音色线索可能泄露说话人身份和社会人口特征，存在隐私泄露和公平性问题

Method: 使用多种匿名化系统，通过说话人验证、口音验证和口音分类任务，分析音色、非音色和口音相关嵌入，评估残留口音信息对再识别的贡献

Result: 部分系统能抵抗口音线索的再识别，但其他系统残留说话人依赖的口音线索可泄露身份；匿名化性能存在口音依赖性公平问题；字符级条件系统能混淆身份揭示性口音线索

Conclusion: 语音匿名化需要考虑口音等非音色线索，字符级条件系统能有效减少口音识别准确率68%，提升匿名化性能11%，改善隐私保护和公平性

Abstract: Voice anonymisation is used to conceal voice identity while preserving linguistic content. Even if anonymisation seems strong, non-timbral cues such as accent that remain post-anonymisation can help re-identification and reveal sensitive socio-demographic traits. We report a study of residual accent information involving multiple anonymisation systems. We highlight the role of accent using speaker verification, accent verification, and accent classification using a set of embeddings focusing on timbral, non-timbral and accent-related information and show the extent to which related cues facilitate reidentification post anonymisation. Results show that, while some systems are robust to reidentification attempts using accent cues, others leave residual, speaker-dependent, accentrelated cues which can be used to reveal the voice identity. We also highlight accent-dependent variation in anonymisation performance, raising fairness concerns, and show that a system with characterlevel conditioning can help obfuscate identity-revealing accent cues, reducing accent-identification accuracy by 68% on average and improving overall anonymisation performance by 11% relative.

</details>


### [7] [Gaussian Mixture Model Based Bayesian Learning for Sparse Channel Estimation in Orthogonal Time Frequency Space Modulated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26902)
*Surbhi Gehlot,Suraj Srivastava,Sandeep Kumar Yadav,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于高斯混合模型(GMM)辅助的稀疏贝叶斯学习(SBL)框架，用于OTFS调制系统中的CSI估计，通过分层GMM先验迭代优化后验分布，在延迟多普勒域实现稀疏性，同时有效利用信道统计特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTFS系统中需要高效的信道状态信息估计方法，现有稀疏估计方法未能充分利用信道统计特性，且传统方法需要浪费频谱资源的保护间隔，导致频谱效率低下。

Method: 将CSI恢复建模为SBL推断问题，采用分层GMM先验迭代优化后验分布；使用复高斯混合表示信道统计特性以建模复杂多径衰落场景；利用时域导频避免延迟多普勒域保护间隔；最终将估计的CSI应用于线性MMSE检测器。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法相比现有稀疏估计方法有显著性能提升，实现了低导频开销和高频谱效率，同时推导了Oracle-MMSE和BCRLB作为性能基准。

Conclusion: 提出的GMM辅助SBL框架能够有效利用信道统计特性，在OTFS系统中实现高性能CSI估计，为可靠数据检测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: A novel Gaussian mixture model (GMM) aided sparse Bayesian learning (SBL) framework is proposed for channel state information (CSI) estimation in orthogonal time-frequency space (OTFS) modulated systems. The key attribute of the proposed algorithm lies in casting CSI recovery as an SBL inference problem, where posterior distributions are iteratively refined under a hierarchical GMM prior. Using this approach, the sparsity-inducing variances beneficially promote sparsity in the delay Doppler (DD) domain, while additionally augmenting the capability of SBL to exploit channel statistics more effectively. Moreover, to fully exploit the GMMs ability to approximate arbitrary probability density functions and model complex multipath fading scenarios, the channel statistics are represented using a complex Gaussian mixture. Simultaneously, the method leverages time-domain (TD) pilots without requiring wasteful DD domain guard intervals, thereby ensuring low pilot overhead and high spectral efficiency. The CSI recovered is subsequently applied in a linear minimum mean square error (MMSE) detector for reliable data detection. To benchmark performance, the Oracle-MMSE and the Bayesian Cramèr Rao lower bound (BCRLB) are also derived. Our simulation results demonstrate significant performance improvement over the state of the art sparse estimation methods.

</details>


### [8] [A Unified Codebook Design for Curvature-Reconfigurable Apertures: Seamless Near to Far Field Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27068)
*Zhoujie You,Shu Sun,Ruifeng Gao,Jue Wang,Xianghao Yu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种用于曲率可重构孔径阵列的统一距离自适应分层码本框架，解决近场和远场场景中的角度-距离耦合问题，无需硬模式切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极大规模阵列的波束训练面临严重且几何依赖的角度-距离耦合挑战。现有设计大多将近场和远场场景分开处理，需要一种统一的解决方案来桥接两种传播机制。

Method: 基于球面波模型，在极角域表征波束成形增益相关性，推导角度相关的角度采样规则以捕捉变化的曲率。引入方向相关的有效瑞利距离作为软边界来门控距离采样，通过在倒数距离域均匀采样实现精确聚焦。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该统一设计在频谱效率和对准精度方面持续优于代表性基线方法，为全范围CuRA通信提供了全面解决方案。

Conclusion: 提出的统一距离自适应分层码本框架能够无缝桥接近场和远场操作，消除硬模式切换需求，为曲率可重构孔径阵列提供高效的全范围波束训练方案。

Abstract: Beam training for extremely large-scale arrays with curvature-reconfigurable apertures (CuRAs) faces the critical challenge of severe, geometry-dependent angle-range coupling. While most existing designs compartmentalize near field and far field scenarios, we propose a unified, distance-adaptive hierarchical codebook framework for 1-D and 2-D CuRAs that seamlessly bridges both propagation regimes. Under a spherical-wave model, we first characterize the beamforming-gain correlation in a polar angular domain, deriving an angle-dependent angular sampling rule to capture the varying curvature. To achieve full-range coverage, we introduce a direction-dependent effective Rayleigh distance (ERD) as a soft boundary to gate the range sampling. Crucially, by sampling uniformly in the reciprocal-range domain, the proposed codebook provides precise, dense focusing within the ERD and automatically degenerates into sparse, angle-only steering beyond it. This mechanism eliminates the need for hard mode-switching between near- and far-field operations. Simulation results demonstrate that our unified design consistently outperforms representative baselines in spectral efficiency and alignment accuracy, offering a comprehensive solution for full-range CuRA communications.

</details>


### [9] [Switch-DFT: Adaptive Waveform and MIMO Switching for Energy-Efficient Base Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27192)
*Jaebum Park,Chan-Byoung Chae,Robert W. Heath*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出Switch-DFT自适应切换框架，通过波形（CP-OFDM/DFT-s-OFDM）和模式（SIMO/MIMO）的动态选择来提升基站能效，减少功率放大器回退并降低功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基站中功率放大器消耗大量能量且效率有限，能效成为关键挑战。需要开发自适应技术来优化能量使用。

Method: 提出Switch-DFT自适应切换框架，根据信道条件动态选择波形（CP-OFDM或DFT-s-OFDM）和传输模式（SIMO或MIMO），通过DFT-s-OFDM减少功率放大器回退，利用MIMO提高吞吐量。

Result: Switch-DFT在广泛的频谱效率范围内相比静态配置展现出更优的能效表现，能够以更低功耗实现目标速率。

Conclusion: 波形和模式自适应切换是提升基站能效的有效方法，Switch-DFT框架通过智能选择最佳配置显著改善能量效率。

Abstract: Energy efficiency has emerged as a critical challenge in modern base stations (BSs), as the power amplifier (PA) consumes a substantial portion of the total power due to its limited efficiency. We investigate waveform and mode adaptation to enhance the energy efficiency of BSs. We propose Switch-DFT, an adaptive switching framework that selects between cyclic prefix orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (CP-OFDM) and discrete Fourier transform-spread-OFDM (DFT-s-OFDM) waveforms, as well as between single-input multiple-output (SIMO) and multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) modes. Switch-DFT improves efficiency by reducing PA backoff with DFT-s-OFDM and achieves the target rate at lower power by leveraging higher MIMO throughput. This results in superior energy efficiency over a wide range of the spectral efficiencies compared with static configurations.

Abstract: In this work, we propose a zero constellation for binary modulation on conjugate-reciprocal zeros (BMOCZ), called jutted BMOCZ (J-BMOCZ), and study its application to non-coherent orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM). With J-BMOCZ, we introduce asymmetry to the zero constellation for Huffman BMOCZ, which removes ambiguity at the receiver under a uniform rotation of the zeros. The asymmetry is controlled by the magnitude of "jutted" zeros and enables the receiver to estimate zero rotation using a simple cross-correlation. The proposed method, however, leads to a natural trade-off between asymmetry and zero stability. Accordingly, we introduce a reliability metric to measure the stability of a polynomial's zeros under an additive perturbation of the coefficients, and we apply the metric to optimize the J-BMOCZ zero constellation parameters. We then combine the advantages of J-BMOCZ and Huffman BMOCZ to design a hybrid waveform for OFDM with BMOCZ (OFDM-BMOCZ). The pilot-free waveform enables blind synchronization/detection and has a fixed peak-to-average power ratio that is independent of the message. Finally, we assess the proposed scheme through simulation and demonstrate non-coherent OFDM-BMOCZ using low-cost software-defined radios.

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了一个MA辅助DoA估计的速度剖面优化框架，有效平衡了感知精度和机械能耗，提出的谱离散化和凸优化方法能够高效求解无限维优化问题，为实际MA系统设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Movable antennas (MAs) enable the reconfiguration of array geometry within a bounded region to exploit sub-wavelength spatial degrees of freedom in wireless communication and sensing systems. However, most prior research has predominantly focused on the communication and sensing performance, overlooking the mechanical power consumption inherent in antenna movement. To bridge this gap, this paper investigates a velocity profile optimization framework for MA-assisted direction-of-arrival (DoA) estimation, explicitly balancing sensing accuracy with mechanical energy consumption of MAs. We first establish a Newtonian-based mechanical energy model, and formulate a functional optimization problem for sensing energy efficiency (EE) maximization. By applying the calculus of variations, this formulation is transformed into an infinite-dimensional problem defined by the Euler-Lagrange equation. To solve it, we propose a spectral discretization framework based on the Galerkin method, which expands the velocity profile over a sinusoidal basis. In the regime where energy consumption is dominated by linear damping, we prove that the optimal velocity profile follows a closed-form sinusoidal shape. For more general scenarios involving strong nonlinear aerodynamic drag, we leverage the Markov-Lukács theorem to transform the kinematic constraints into strictly convex sum-of-squares (SOS) conditions. Consequently, the infinite-dimensional problem is reformulated as a tractable finite-dimensional nonlinear algebraic system, which is solved by a two-layer algorithm combining Dinkelbach's method with successive convex approximation (SCA). Numerical results demonstrate that our optimized velocity profile significantly outperforms baselines in terms of EE across various system configurations. Insights into the optimized velocity profiles and practical design guidelines are also provided.

</details>

Abstract: Reconfigurable intelligent surfaces have emerged as a cutting-edge technology for next-generation wireless communications that are capable of reconfiguring the wireless environment using a large number of cost-effective reflecting elements. However, a significant body of prior studies has focused on single-layer surfaces that lack the capability of significantly mitigating inter-user interference. Moreover, previous studies mostly consider far-field operation and neglect working in the near-field region. In this paper, we propose a stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIM)-assisted near-field multi-user multiple-input-single-output covert communication system. More specifically, we have a multi-antenna base station that is assisted with a SIM to serve multiple single-antenna users in the presence of multiple single-antenna wardens. We aim at optimizing the beamfocusing vectors at the BS and SIM phase shift matrices to maximize the sum covert rate under maximum transmit power budget constraint, quality-of-service (QoS) constraint for all users, and covertness constraint. Since the formulated problem is highly non-convex due to the coupling between the variables, we adopt alternating optimization to tackle it, where we divide the problem into beamfocusing sub-problem and SIM phase shift sub-problem, which are solved alternately until convergence. We leverage successive convex approximation (SCA) to solve the two sub-problems. Additionally, we formulate the SIM phase shift sub-problem using the widely adopted projected gradient ascent (PGA) method for comparison purposes. The conducted simulations reveal that the SCA-based algorithm outperforms the existing PGA-based algorithm as well as other benchmarks in terms of the achieved sum covert rate, demonstrating its consistent performance and robustness under various system parameter configurations.

</details>

Abstract: This paper investigates the physical layer security of a non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) system assisted by a tertiary-mode simultaneously transmitting and reflecting reconfigurable intelligent surface (STAR-RIS), which can perform transmission, reflection, and jamming simultaneously. The system comprises a base station (BS) serving two users located on opposite sides of the STAR-RIS, assuming perfect channel state information (CSI) at the transmitter. To enhance secrecy performance, a subset of STAR-RIS elements is adaptively configured for jamming. A penalty-based alternating optimization algorithm is developed to jointly optimize the BS's active beamforming and the STAR-RIS's passive beamforming and mode selection. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed design substantially improves the achievable sum rate and secrecy performance compared to conventional RIS-assisted and no-RIS benchmarks, highlighting the potential of tertiary-mode STAR-RIS for secure and efficient next-generation wireless communications.

Abstract: To advance integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) in sixth-generation (6G) extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (XL-MIMO) networks, a low-complexity compressed sensing (CS)-based dictionary design is proposed for wideband near-field (WB-NF) target localization. Currently, the massive signal dimensions in the WB-NF regime impose severe computational burdens and high spatial-frequency coherence on conventional grid-based algorithms. Furthermore, a unified framework exploiting both wideband (WB) and near-field (NF) effects is lacking, and the analytical conditions for simplifying this model into decoupled approximations remain uncharacterized. To address these challenges, the proposed algorithm mathematically decouples the mutual coherence function and introduces a novel angle-distance sampling grid with customized distance adjustments, drastically reducing dictionary dimensions while ensuring low coherence. To isolate the individual WB and NF impacts, two coherence-based metrics are formulated to establish the effective boundaries of the narrowband near-field (NB-NF) and wideband far-field (WB-FF) regions, where respective multiple signal classification (MUSIC) algorithms are utilized. Simulations demonstrate that the CS-based method achieves robust performance across the entire regime, and the established boundaries provide crucial theoretical guidelines for WB and NF effect decoupling.

Abstract: We study joint compression and detection in distributed sensing systems motivated by emerging applications such as IoT-based localization. Two spatially separated sensors observe noisy signals and can exchange only a $k$-bit message over a reliable one-way low-rate link. One sensor compresses its observation into a $k$-bit description to help the other decide whether their observations share a common underlying signal or are statistically independent. We propose a simple extremum-based strategy, in which the encoder sends the index of its largest sample and the decoder performs a scalar threshold test. We derive exact nonasymptotic false-alarm and misdetection probabilities and validate the analysis with representative simulations.

</details>

Abstract: We revisit the recently proposed ``self-Wiener" (SW) filtering method for robust deconvolution, and generalize it to the classical denoising problem. The resulting estimator, termed generalized SW (GSW) filtering, retains the nonlinear shrinkage structure of SW but introduces a tunable threshold parameter. This tunability enables GSW to flexibly adapt to varying signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) regimes by balancing noise suppression and signal preservation. We derive closed-form expressions for its mean-square error (MSE) performance in both low- and high-SNR regimes, and demonstrate that GSW closely approximates the oracle MMSE at high SNR while maintaining strong robustness at low SNR. Simulation results validate the analytical findings, showing that GSW consistently achieves favorable denoising performance across a wide range of SNRs. Its analytical tractability, parameter flexibility, and close connection to the optimal Wiener filter structure make it a promising tool for practical applications including compressive sensing, sparse signal recovery, and domain-specific shrinkage in wavelet, Fourier, and potentially learned orthonormal representations.

### [17] [iBEAMS: A Unified Framework for Secure and Energy-Efficient ISAC-MIMO Systems leveraging Bayesian Enhanced learning, and Adaptive Game-Theoretic Multi-Layer Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27882)

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: iBEAMS：一种用于分布式混合节点安全节能ISAC的分层Stackelberg-GNE-贝叶斯框架，在毫米波和太赫兹频段实现物理层保密，协调边缘节点并满足功率与QoS约束。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代毫米波/太赫兹ISAC网络需要同时解决物理层保密、分布式混合节点协调、严格功率和QoS约束等挑战，而现有ISAC物理层安全设计很少统一处理这些要求。

Method: 提出iBEAMS分层框架：1) Stackelberg领导者（ISAC基站）联合优化总发射功率、保密数据/人工噪声/感知的功率分配，广播激励价格；2) 广义纳什均衡博弈，混合节点在耦合干扰约束和泄漏惩罚下选择发射功率与传输/干扰角色；3) 贝叶斯协同优化层，基于窃听者到达角后验分布形成几何感知的干扰联盟。

Result: 在28 GHz至3 THz载频仿真中，iBEAMS实现约4.4-4.7 bps/Hz平均保密速率，保密能效比Stackelberg决策基线提高约2倍，比另一基线提高30-70%，在28 GHz保持零中断。后验对齐干扰在移动窃听者和增加对手密度下保持高指向性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: iBEAMS能同时对抗静态和移动对手，在有限功率和QoS约束下协调混合边缘节点，实现安全节能的ISAC网络，为下一代毫米波/太赫兹ISAC提供统一解决方案。

Abstract: Next generation ISAC networks operating in the mmWave and THz bands must provide physical layer secrecy against potential eavesdroppers (mobile and static) while coordinating distributed hybrid edge nodes under stringent power and QoS constraints. However, these requirements are rarely addressed in a unified manner in existing ISAC physical layer security designs. This paper proposes iBEAMS, a hierarchical Stackelberg--GNE--Bayesian framework for secure and energy efficient ISAC with distributed hybrid nodes. The proposed architecture integrates: (i) a Stackelberg leader at the ISAC base station that jointly optimizes total transmit power, power splitting among confidential data, artificial noise, and sensing, and broadcasts incentive prices to shape follower utilities; (ii) a Generalized Nash Equilibrium Game in which hybrid nodes select transmit powers and transmission versus jamming roles under coupled interference constraints and base-station-imposed leakage penalties; and (iii) a Bayesian cooperative refinement layer that forms geometry-aware jamming coalitions aligned with the posterior distribution of the eavesdropper's Angle of Arrival. Simulations over carrier frequencies from 28 GHz to 3 THz demonstrate hierarchical convergence of both base station and hybrid node decisions with stable cooperative friendly jamming. iBEAMS attains approximately 4.4--4.7 bps/Hz average secrecy rate, achieves about $2\times$ higher Secrecy Energy Efficiency (SEE), and delivers 30--70% higher SEE than a Stackelberg-decision-based baseline, while maintaining zero outage at 28 GHz. Moreover, the posterior-aligned jamming remains sharply directive and resilient under mobile eavesdroppers and increasing adversary density, indicating that iBEAMS can simultaneously act against static and mobile adversaries while coordinating hybrid edge nodes under limited power and QoS constraints.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署大语言模型可以减少交互服务的延迟，但用户设备在基站间切换时会导致服务中断。为了及时恢复解码，目标侧边缘服务器需要恢复用户上下文状态，这可以通过令牌转发加预填充计算或直接通过回传传输KV缓存来实现

Method: 提出统一的切换设计，联合选择预填充长度并调度回传KV缓存传输，以最小化多用户LLM切换的最差用户延迟。该方法采用可处理的逐步解决方案，具有明确的可行性条件和构造性的速率调度策略

Result: 仿真表明，所提方法在各种回传容量、预填充速度和上下文大小下始终优于基线方法，为移动感知的边缘LLM令牌流提供了实用指导

Conclusion: 该研究为移动感知的边缘LLM令牌流提供了实用的切换设计指南，通过优化预填充和KV缓存传输的联合调度，有效减少了切换延迟

Abstract: Edge deployment of large language models (LLMs) can reduce latency for interactive services, but mobility introduces service interruptions when an user equipment (UE) hands over between base stations (BSs). To promptly resume decoding, the target-side edge server must recover the UE context state, which can be provisioned either by token forwarding followed by prefill computation or by direct key-value (KV) cache transmission over backhaul. This paper proposes a unified handover (HO) design that jointly selects the prefill length and schedules backhaul KV cache delivery to minimize the worst-user LLM HO delay for multiple UEs. The resulting scheme admits a tractable step-wise solution with explicit feasibility conditions and a constructive rate-scheduling policy. Simulations show that the proposed method consistently outperforms baselines across a wide range of backhaul capacities, prefill speeds, and context sizes, providing practical guidelines for mobility-aware Edge LLM token streaming.

</details>

### [20] [Joint Time-Phase Synchronization for Distributed Sensing Networks via Feature-Level Hyper-Plane Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28121)
*Kailun Tian,Kaili Jiang,Dechang Wang,Yuxin Zhao,Yuxin Shang,Hancong Feng,Bin Tang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于广义超平面回归的联合校准框架，解决分布式物联网感知网络中的时钟偏移和射频相位误差同步问题，实现皮秒级同步精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式物联网感知网络需要精确同步来联合补偿时钟偏移和射频相位误差，但传统的两步协议存在时间-相位耦合问题，残余定时偏移会降低相位相干性。

Method: 提出广义超平面回归框架，将耦合的时空相位演化转化为统一回归模型，实现参数解耦；采用线性调频波形降低模型阶数；设计特征级分布式架构和单向特征传输机制，减少通信开销。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在千米级分布式物联网感知网络中，即使在严重噪声条件下，也能实现可靠的皮秒级同步精度。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了分布式物联网感知网络中的时间-相位耦合问题，通过联合校准和资源优化设计，为资源受限的物联网边缘节点提供了可行的同步解决方案。

Abstract: Achieving coherent integration in distributed Internet of Things (IoT) sensing networks requires precise synchronization to jointly compensate clock offsets and radio-frequency (RF) phase errors. Conventional two-step protocols suffer from time-phase coupling, where residual timing offsets degrade phase coherence. This paper proposes a generalized hyper-plane regression (GHR) framework for joint calibration by transforming coupled spatiotemporal phase evolution into a unified regression model, enabling effective parameter decoupling. To support resource-constrained IoT edge nodes, a feature-level distributed architecture is developed. By adopting a linear frequency-modulated (LFM) waveform, the model order is reduced, yielding linear computational complexity. In addition, a unidirectional feature transmission mechanism eliminates the communication overhead of bidirectional timestamp exchange, making the approach suitable for resource-constrained IoT networks. Simulation results demonstrate reliable picosecond-level synchronization accuracy under severe noise across kilometer-scale distributed IoT sensing networks.

</details>


### [21] [Weighted Sum-Rate Maximization for RIS-UAV-assisted Space-Air-Ground Integrated Network with RSMA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28176)
*Jian He,Cong Zhou,Shuo Shi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于速率分割多址接入的天地一体化网络联合优化框架，通过波束成形、RIS-UAV部署与相位调整、速率分割等联合优化最大化加权和速率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天地一体化网络中卫星、基站、无人机等节点共存导致相互干扰，需要设计有效的资源分配方案来提升系统性能

Method: 采用块坐标下降法分解问题，结合WMMSE方法处理非凸目标函数，针对速率分割提出贪心算法，波束成形使用逐次凸逼近，RIS相位采用ADMM算法，UAV位置和姿态使用穷举搜索

Result: 仿真结果表明所提算法在用户加权和速率方面实现了优越性能

Conclusion: 提出的RSMA框架和联合优化算法能有效提升SAGIN网络性能，为解决复杂网络中的干扰问题提供了有效方案

Abstract: In this paper, a rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) based joint optimization framework for the space-air-ground integrated network (SAGIN) is proposed, where the satellite and base stations employ uniform planar array (UPA) antennas for signal transmission, and unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) relay the satellite signals. Earth stations (ESs) and user equipments (UEs) receive signals from satellite and base stations (BSs), respectively, resulting in mutual interference. We first model the channels and signals in this scenario and analyse the interference at BSs and UEs. Then, We formulate a joint optimization problem aimed at maximizing the weighted sum-rate, involving beamforming, RIS-UAV deployment and phase shifts, and rate splitting. However, this problem is highly non-convex. To tackle this challenge, we apply a block coordinate descent (BCD) approach to decompose the problem and employ the weighted minimum mean square error (WMMSE) method to transform the non-convex objective function. For the rate-splitting sub-problem, a greedy algorithm is proposed and a successive convex approximation (SCA) algorithm is used for beamforming. Besides, the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) algorithm is employed for the RIS phase-shift problem with unit-modulus constraints, and an exhaustive search method is adopted for the complex UAV positioning and orientation. Simulation results validate that the proposed algorithm achieves superior performance in terms of user weighted sum-rate.

</details>


### [22] [Toward Multi-Satellite Cooperative Transmission: A Joint Framework for CSI Acquisition, Feedback, and Phase Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28195)
*Yiming Zhu,Yafei Wang,Carla Amatetti,Alessandro Vanelli-Coralli,Wenjin Wang,Rui Ding,Symeon Chatzinotas,Björn Ottersten*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出JCAFPS框架解决多卫星协作传输中的同步问题，通过联合CSI获取、反馈和相位级同步，实现接近理论6dB功率增益的分布式预编码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单卫星传输受限于链路预算，多卫星协作传输（如分布式预编码）需要严格的时频相同步，但在快速信道变化和反馈延迟下难以维持，需要新的同步解决方案。

Method: 提出JCAFPS框架：1）设计波束域可调相移跟踪参考信号传输方案；2）建立延迟和多普勒频移的多项式时变模型；3）开发闭环实现，集成信道参数估计、预测、反馈和用户特定时频相预补偿。

Result: 数值结果表明，该框架能实现准确的CSI获取和精确的时频相同步，使基于分布式预编码的双卫星协作传输接近理论6dB功率增益，且在延长预测时间和增大TRS周期下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: JCAFPS框架有效解决了多卫星协作传输中的同步挑战，通过联合CSI获取、反馈和相位级同步，实现了接近理论极限的协作增益，为实际LEO星座系统提供了可行的同步解决方案。

Abstract: The stringent link budget, caused by long propagation distances and payload constraints, poses a fundamental bottleneck for single-satellite transmission. Although LEO mega-constellations make multi-satellite cooperative transmission (MSCT), such as distributed precoding (DP), increasingly feasible, its cooperative gains critically rely on stringent time-frequency-phase synchronization (TFP-Sync), which is difficult to maintain under rapid channel variation and feedback latency. To address this issue, this paper proposes a joint CSI acquisition, feedback, and phase-level synchronization (JCAFPS) framework for MSCT. Specifically, to enable reliable, overhead-efficient CSI acquisition, we design a beam-domain adjustable phase-shift tracking reference signal (TRS) transmission scheme, along with criteria for the TRS and CSI-feedback periods. Then, exploiting deterministic orbital motion and dominant LoS propagation, we establish a polynomial model for the temporal evolution of delay and Doppler shift, and derive an OFDM-based multi-satellite signal model under non-ideal synchronization. The analysis reveals that, unlike the single-satellite case, the composite multi-satellite channel exhibits nonlinear time-frequency-varying phase behavior, necessitating symbol- and subcarrier-wise phase precompensation for coherent transmission. Based on these results, we develop a practical closed-loop realization integrating single-TRS-based channel parameter estimation, multi-TRS-based channel prediction, predictive CSI feedback, and user-specific TFP precompensation. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves accurate CSI acquisition and precise TFP-Sync, enabling DP-based dual-satellite cooperative transmission to approach the theoretical 6 dB power gain over single-satellite transmission, while remaining robust under extended prediction durations and enlarged TRS periods.

</details>


### [23] [Spectral Segmented Linear Regression for Coarse Carrier Frequency Offset Estimation in Optical LEO Satellite Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28262)
*I. P. Vieira,G. V. Serra,R. A. Colares,D. A. A. Mello*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种用于相干光通信系统的鲁棒非数据辅助宽范围载波频率偏移估计算法，特别针对低符号率、大频偏和低信噪比场景，如卫星光通信链路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全数字CFO估计方法在典型光纤通信链路中可靠，但在低符号率、大载波频偏（接近信号带宽）和严重加性噪声场景下性能受限。这在光学地面站与低轨卫星链路中尤为突出，多普勒频移可达数GHz，大气衰减严重，传统方法可能失效。

Method: 提出鲁棒的非数据辅助宽范围CFO估计方案，作为粗CFO估计算法。通过对接收信号频谱进行低复杂度操作，将频率估计任务重构为分段线性回归问题，部分补偿CFO，使后续精细CFO估计算法能够工作。

Result: 数值仿真在压力测试场景（大CFO、低SNR、低符号率）中显示，该方法具有良好的估计精度和鲁棒收敛性。实验离线验证进一步证实了该方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法为解决卫星光通信等恶劣条件下的载波频率偏移估计问题提供了一种有效的解决方案，能够在大频偏、低信噪比环境下实现鲁棒估计。

Abstract: Carrier frequency offset estimation (CFOE) is a critical stage in modern coherent optical communication systems. Although conventional all-digital techniques perform reliably in typical fiber-optic communication links, CFOE often becomes a major bottleneck in low-symbol-rate scenarios with large carrier CFOs (approaching the signal bandwidth) and severe additive noise levels. These conditions are particularly prevalent in links between optical ground stations (OGSs) and low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites, where Doppler-induced frequency shifts of several gigahertz and atmospheric attenuation significantly degrade CFOE performance and can render traditional methods ineffective. In this paper, we propose a robust non-data-aided (NDA) scheme designed for wide-range CFOE. Such a coarse CFOE (C-CFOE) algorithm partially compensates for the CFO, enabling the operation of a subsequent fine CFOE algorithm. By applying low-complexity operations to the spectrum of the received signal, we recast the frequency estimation task as a segmented linear regression (SLR) problem. Numerical simulations in stress-test scenarios involving large CFOs, low SNR, and low symbol rates show that the proposed approach achieves good estimation accuracy and robust convergence. Experimental offline validation further confirms the practical feasibility of the method.

</details>


### [24] [Clustered Movable Pinching Antennas: Realizing Beamforming Gains and Target Diversity in ISAC Systems with Look-Angle-Dependent RCS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28264)
*Ata Khalili,Brikena Kaziu,Vasilis K. Papanikolaou,Robert Schober*

Main category: eess.SP

Result: 该系统能够同时实现精确的波束成形和从多个角度对目标进行照射，为集成感知与通信提供了新的硬件架构解决方案

Conclusion: 基于可移动夹持天线簇的ISAC系统设计为同时优化通信和感知性能提供了创新方案，通过天线移动和空间分离实现了波束成形和多角度感知的协同

Abstract: We investigate a novel integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) system enabled by pinching antennas (PAs), which are dynamically activated along a dielectric waveguide. Unlike prior designs, the PAs are organized into multiple clusters of movable antennas. The movement of the antennas within each cluster enables transmit beamforming, while the spatial separation of different clusters allows the system to illuminate the target from multiple angular perspectives.

</details>


### [25] [Multimodal-NF: A Wireless Dataset for Near-Field Low-Altitude Sensing and Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28280)
*Mengyuan Li,Qianfan Lu,Jiachen Tian,Hongjun Hu,Yu Han,Xiao Li,Chao-Kai Wen,Shi Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Multimodal-NF是一个用于6G无线网络的大规模数据集和生成框架，同步提供近场信道状态信息、无线标签与多模态感知数据（RGB图像、LiDAR点云、GPS），以支持环境感知的低空XL-MIMO系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集大多局限于2D地面远场场景，缺乏对低空超大规模MIMO系统至关重要的3D空间上下文和近场特性，无法满足环境感知6G网络对多模态与无线数据深度融合的需求。

Method: 提出Multimodal-NF数据集和专用生成框架，在上中频段运行，同步采集高保真近场信道状态信息、精确无线标签（如前5波束索引、视距/非视距）与全面的感知模态数据（RGB图像、LiDAR点云、GPS）。

Result: 通过代表性案例研究验证了数据集的实用性和有效性，多模态先验提供的空间语义有助于减少近场搜索空间，从而降低无线感知和通信任务的开销。

Conclusion: Multimodal-NF填补了现有数据集的空白，为环境感知6G无线网络提供了重要的数据集和生成工具，支持低空XL-MIMO系统的研究和开发。

Abstract: Environment-aware 6G wireless networks demand the deep integration of multimodal and wireless data. However, most existing datasets are confined to 2D terrestrial far-field scenarios, lacking the 3D spatial context and near-field characteristics crucial for low-altitude extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (XL-MIMO) systems. To bridge this gap, this letter introduces Multimodal-NF, a large-scale dataset and specialized generation framework. Operating in the upper midband, it synchronizes high-fidelity near-field channel state information (CSI) and precise wireless labels (e.g., Top-5 beam indices, LoS/NLoS) with comprehensive sensory modalities (RGB images, LiDAR point clouds, and GPS). Crucially, these multimodal priors provide spatial semantics that help reduce the near-field search space and thereby lower the overhead of wireless sensing and communication tasks. Finally, we validate the dataset through representative case studies, demonstrating its utility and effectiveness. The open-source generator and dataset are available at https://lmyxxn.github.io/6GXLMIMODatasets/.

</details>


### [26] [Distributed User Scheduling in Multi-Cell MIMO O-RAN with QoS Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28283)
*Tenghao Cai,Lei Li,Tsung-Hui Chang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了O-RAN中多载波资源块组调度问题，针对MU-MIMO系统同时包含JT和非JT用户的情况，提出了集中式和分布式调度方案，分布式方案在保持接近集中式性能的同时显著降低了复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN采用分布式架构，需要支持MU-MIMO、载波聚合和联合传输等先进物理层技术。多载波RBG调度在同时包含JT和非JT用户的MU-MIMO系统中面临挑战，需要解决变量耦合、非凸性和组合复杂性等问题，同时满足O-RAN架构的分布式需求。

Method: 1) 将特征基零迫收发器设计扩展到JT用户；2) 利用大规模MIMO渐近特性推导可处理的分离速率近似；3) 开发两种解决方案：集中式块坐标下降基准算法和符合O-RAN架构的分布式调度器；4) 分布式方案仅需一轮轻量级小区间协调。

Result: 提出的分布式调度器在保持接近集中式性能的同时，显著降低了复杂度和延迟。大规模仿真验证表明，该方案具有高可扩展性、快速收敛性和更好的QoS满足率。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了O-RAN中多载波RBG调度问题，提出的分布式调度方案符合O-RAN架构要求，在性能、复杂度和延迟之间取得了良好平衡，为大规模MU-MIMO网络提供了有效的调度解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed scheduling is essential for open radio access network (O-RAN) employing advanced physical-layer techniques such as multi-user MIMO (MU-MIMO), carrier aggregation (CA), and joint transmission (JT). This work investigates the multi-component-carrier (multi-CC) resource block group (RBG) scheduling in MU-MIMO O-RAN with both JT and non-JT users. We formulate a scheduling optimization problem to maximize throughput subject to user-specific quality of service (QoS) requirements while ensuring consistent allocations across cooperating O-RAN radio units (O-RUs) required by JT transmission. The strong variable coupling, non-convexity, and combinatorial complexity make the problem highly challenging. To tackle this, we extend the eigen-based zero-forcing transceiver design to JT users and leverage massive MIMO asymptotic properties to derive a tractable, separable rate approximation. Building on this, we develop two solutions: a centralized block coordinate descent benchmark and a distributed scheduler aligned with the O-RAN architecture. The proposed distributed scheme achieves near-centralized performance with only one round of lightweight coordination among cells, significantly reducing complexity and delay. Extensive simulations validate that our distributed scheduler achieves high scalability, fast convergence, and better QoS satisfaction rate in large-scale MU-MIMO networks.

</details>


### [27] [Toward Distributed User Scheduling and Coordinated Beamforming in Multi-Cell mmWave Networks: A Sensing-Assisted Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28305)
*Tenghao Cai,Lei Li,Shutao Zhang,Tsung-Hui Chang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于感知辅助的分布式用户调度与协调波束成形框架，利用ISAC回波感知用户位置，通过信道知识地图将位置映射到CSI估计，显著减少基站间信息交换开销，提升毫米波网络吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统协调干扰管理受限于计算复杂度和回程资源约束，分布式用户调度与协调波束成形面临获取小区间信道状态信息的实际挑战，基站通常只能进行信号强度测量，而高维CSI交换会产生大量开销。

Method: 提出感知辅助的分布式用户调度与协调波束成形框架：1) 利用信道知识地图将用户位置映射到CSI估计，用户位置通过ISAC回波主动感知；2) 采用信号与平均泄漏加干扰加噪声比指标进行分布式ISAC波束成形优化，基站同时与用户通信并感知其位置；3) 设计高效的分布式用户调度和ISAC波束成形算法联合优化通信和感知性能。

Result: 广泛的数值结果表明网络吞吐量得到显著提升，验证了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过仅交换用户位置信息实现分布式协调传输，大幅减少回程开销，为多小区毫米波网络提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Providing guaranteed quality of service for cell-edge users remains a longstanding challenge in wireless networks. While coordinated interference management was proposed decades ago, its potential has been limited by computational complexity and backhaul resource constraints. Distributed user scheduling and coordinated beamforming (D-USCB) offers a scalable solution but faces practical challenges in acquiring inter-cell channel state information (CSI), as base stations (BSs) are often restricted to signal strength measurements, and high-dimensional CSI exchange incurs substantial overhead. Inspired by integrated sensing and communication (ISAC), this paper proposes a sensing-assisted D-USCB (SD-USCB) framework to maximize the network throughput of multi-cell mmWave networks. Firstly, the framework leverages channel knowledge maps (CKMs) that map user locations to CSI estimates, where user locations are proactively sensed via ISAC echoes. Secondly, we employ a signal-to-average-leakage-plus-interference-plus-noise ratio (SALINR) metric for distributed ISAC beamforming optimization, in which BSs simultaneously communicate with users and sense their locations. These two components jointly enable distributed coordinated transmission with only user location information exchanged among BSs, thereby substantially reducing backhaul overhead. In addition, we devise efficient distributed user scheduling and ISAC beamforming algorithms to jointly optimize communication and sensing performance. Extensive numerical results demonstrate significant improvements in network throughput, validating the efficacy of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [28] [Integrated sensing and communications in the 3GPP New Radio: sensing limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28318)
*Santiago Fernńdez,Javier Giménez,Mari Carmen Aguayo-Torres,José A. Cortés*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 分析5G NR信号结构在ISAC中的感知性能极限，通过CRLB理论分析发现需要多时隙估计才能满足3GPP精度要求，并提出改进的两步迭代估计算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究当前5G NR信号结构是否能够满足3GPP标准中ISAC的感知精度要求，特别是在无人机单站感知应用场景下。

Method: 通过推导Cramér-Rao下界(CRLB)理论分析距离和速度估计的基本极限，评估标准化信号性能，并提出两步迭代距离和径向速度估计算法。

Result: 5G NR PRS在单时隙资源下对速度估计可能不是最优的，但采用多时隙估计后变得合适；提出的两步迭代估计器比传统ML估计器在更宽距离范围内达到CRLB。

Conclusion: 需要利用多时隙信息才能满足3GPP性能目标，提出的估计算法显著扩展了满足精度要求的距离范围，为5G/6G ISAC系统设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communications (ISAC) is regarded as a key element of the beyond-fifth-generation (5G) and sixth-generation (6G) systems, raising the question of whether current 5G New Radio (NR) signal structures can meet the sensing accuracy requirements specified by the Third Generation Partnership Project (3GPP). This paper addresses this issue by analyzing the fundamental limits of range and velocity estimation through the Cramér-Rao lower bound (CRLB) for a monostatic unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) sensing use case currently under consideration in the 3GPP standardization process. The study focuses on standardized signals and also evaluates the potential performance gains achievable with reference signals specifically designed for sensing purposes.
  The compact CRLB expressions derived in this work highlight the fundamental trade-offs between estimation accuracy and system parameters. The results further indicate that information from multiple slots must be exploited in the estimation process to attain the performance targets defined by the 3GPP. As a result, the 5G NR positioning reference signal (PRS), whose patterns may be suboptimal for velocity estimation when using single-slot resources, becomes suitable when multislot estimation is employed. Finally, we propose a two-step iterative range and radial-velocity estimator that attains the CRLB over a significantly wider range of distances than conventional maximum-likelihood (ML) estimators, for which the well-known threshold effect severely limits the distance range over which the accuracy requirements imposed by the 3GPP are satisfied.

</details>


### [29] [Joint Energy Efficiency Optimization for Uplink Multiuser Movable Antenna-Based Wireless Systems Assisted by Movable-Element RIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28559)
*Ayda Nodel Hokmabadi,Mohamed Elhattab,Chadi Assi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究移动元件可重构智能表面(ME-RIS)和移动天线基站(MA-BS)辅助的上行多用户系统能量效率优化，通过联合优化后编码器、用户功率、RIS相移、BS天线和RIS元件位置来最大化系统能量效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定天线基站和固定元件RIS系统在能量效率方面存在局限，需要探索通过移动天线和移动RIS元件的位置优化来进一步提升系统性能。

Method: 采用交替优化(AO)框架处理非凸分式问题，结合Dinkelbach方法和逐次凸逼近(SCA)技术解决子问题，联合优化后编码器向量、用户发射功率、RIS相移、BS天线位置和RIS元件位置。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方案相比固定天线基站和固定元件RIS基准方案，实现了显著的能量效率增益。

Conclusion: 移动天线基站和移动元件RIS的联合位置优化能有效提升上行多用户系统的能量效率，为未来无线通信系统设计提供了有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: This paper investigates energy efficiency (EE) optimization for an uplink multiuser system assisted by a movable-element reconfigurable intelligent surface (ME-RIS) and a base station equipped with movable antennas (MA-BS). We jointly optimize the uplink postcoder vectors, user transmit powers, RIS phase shift, and the positions of both the BS antennas and RIS elements to maximize the system EE. The resulting non-convex fractional problem is solved using an alternating optimization (AO) framework, where subproblems are handled via Dinkelbach's method combined with successive convex approximation (SCA). Simulation results show that the proposed scheme achieves significant EE gains over fixed-antenna BS and fixed-element RIS benchmarks.

</details>


### [30] [Deterministic Modeling of Dynamic ISAC Channels in RF Digital Twin Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28736)
*Cesar Montaner,Saúl Fenollosa,Andres Ortega,Hugo Beltrán,Narcis Cardona*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种校准射频数字孪生（RF-DT）的方法，用于动态无线环境中的集成感知与通信（ISAC），通过高分辨率射线追踪和宽带信道探测确保仿真与实测一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在6G时代，集成感知与通信（ISAC）需要在复杂时变环境中进行算法开发和评估，但现有仿真与实测之间存在差距，需要建立准确的射频数字孪生来弥合这一差距。

Method: 结合高分辨率射线追踪和宽带信道探测，在包含单静态和双静态配置、移动用户平台和车辆的城市场景中进行校准验证。

Result: 校准后的RF-DT能够准确再现多径演化、动态散射体和多普勒效应等关键传播效应，与实测结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 准确的几何建模、材料建模、天线模式和漫散射对于实现高频ISAC的逼真仿真至关重要，该校准框架为6G复杂时变环境中的ISAC算法开发和评估提供了可扩展工具。

Abstract: This paper introduces a methodology to calibrate Radio-Frequency Digital Twins (RF-DTs) for Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) in dynamic wireless environments. The approach leverages high-resolution ray tracing in combination with wideband channel sounding to ensure consistency between simulated and measured propagation. The methodology is validated in urban scenarios featuring both mono-static and bi-static configurations, as well as moving user platforms and vehicles. Results show that the calibrated RF-DT reproduces key propagation effects, including multipath evolution, dynamic scatterers, and Doppler-induced signatures, with close agreement to measurements. These findings confirm that accurate geometry, material modeling, antenna patterns, and diffuse scattering are essential for realistic high-frequency ISAC simulation. By bridging the gap between simulation and measurement, the proposed calibration framework provides a scalable tool for developing and evaluating ISAC algorithms in complex, time-varying environments envisioned for 6G.

</details>


### [31] [Spatial Degrees of Freedom and Channel Strength for Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28749)
*Mats Gustafsson,Yaniv Brick*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文在几何框架下分析天线系统的空间自由度(NDoF)和信道强度，提出基于相关算子和几何度量的有效NDoF估计方法，为近场信道模态丰富度评估提供计算高效的工具箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着天线系统向更高频率和更密集部署发展，准确估计空间自由度和信道强度对于阵列设计、逆问题求解和高容量通信系统至关重要。现有方法在紧密间距配置下存在显著偏差，需要更精确的物理基础理论。

Method: 采用相关算子表示法分析收发区域间的信道，研究特征谱特性并将NDoF与谱过渡点关联。同时开发基于几何的渐近估计方法，利用互阴影度量和耦合强度，推导平行线和区域等典型配置的闭式渐近表达式。

Result: 研究表明：在二维和三维设置中，投影长度或面积预测可用模式数量，而耦合强度决定平均特征值水平。紧密间距配置的有效NDoF与谱角存在显著偏差，验证了理论的物理基础。所提方法计算高效，形成近场信道模态丰富度评估的工具箱。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了空间自由度和信道强度的几何分析框架，提供了物理基础扎实且计算高效的理论工具，对阵列设计、逆问题和高容量通信系统具有重要应用价值，特别是在近场信道模态丰富度评估方面。

Abstract: The number of spatial degrees of freedom (NDoF) and channel strength in antenna systems are examined within a geometric framework. Starting from a correlation-operator representation of the channel between transmitter and receiver regions, we analyze the associated eigenspectrum and relate the NDoF to its spectral transition (corner). We compare the spectrum-based effective NDoF and effective rank metrics, clarifying their behavior for both idealized and realistic eigenvalue distributions. In parallel, we develop geometry-based asymptotic estimates in terms of mutual shadow (view) measures and coupling strength. Specifically, we show that while the projected length or area predicts the number of usable modes in two- and three-dimensional settings, the coupling strength determines the average eigenvalue level. Canonical configurations of parallel lines and regions are used to derive closed-form asymptotic expressions for the effective NDoF, revealing significant deviations from the spectral corner in closely spaced configurations. The results illustrate that these are physically grounded. The proposed theory and techniques are computationally efficient and form a toolbox for estimating the modal richness in near-field channels, with implications for array design, inverse problems, and high-capacity communication systems.

</details>

### [32] [Online Learning of Kalman Filtering: From Output to State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27159)
*Lintao Ye,Ankang Zhang,Ming Chi,Bin Du,Jianghai Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于在线优化的统一算法框架，用于解决部分观测线性动力系统中未知系统模型的卡尔曼滤波学习问题，在输出估计场景实现对数遗憾，在状态估计场景通过随机查询实现平方根遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究部分观测线性动力系统中未知系统模型的卡尔曼滤波学习问题，特别是解决状态估计场景这一文献中的开放性问题，探索有限观测条件下的在线学习问题。

Method: 提出基于在线优化的统一算法框架，利用估计误差成本函数的条件强凸性等特性。针对状态估计场景，引入随机查询方案，使算法能够有限访问更丰富的系统状态测量信息。

Result: 输出估计场景实现O(log T)遗憾；状态估计场景证明无法实现亚线性遗憾，但通过随机查询方案可实现O(√T)遗憾，算法能够捕捉查询次数与遗憾之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 提出的算法框架有效解决了卡尔曼滤波学习问题，特别是在状态估计这一开放问题上取得进展，揭示了有限观测条件下在线学习的权衡关系，并通过数值实验验证了算法性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the problem of learning Kalman filtering with unknown system model in partially observed linear dynamical systems. We propose a unified algorithmic framework based on online optimization that can be used to solve both the output estimation and state estimation scenarios. By exploring the properties of the estimation error cost functions, such as conditionally strong convexity, we show that our algorithm achieves a $\log T$-regret in the horizon length $T$ for the output estimation scenario. More importantly, we tackle the more challenging scenario of learning Kalman filtering for state estimation, which is an open problem in the literature. We first characterize a fundamental limitation of the problem, demonstrating the impossibility of any algorithm to achieve sublinear regret in $T$. By further introducing a random query scheme into our algorithm, we show that a $\sqrt{T}$-regret is achievable when rendering the algorithm limited query access to more informative measurements of the system state in practice. Our algorithm and regret readily capture the trade-off between the number of queries and the achieved regret, and shed light on online learning problems with limited observations. We validate the performance of our algorithms using numerical examples.

</details>


### [33] [Interpretable Physics Extraction from Data for Linear Dynamical Systems using Lie Generator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27442)
*Shafayeth Jamil,Rehan Kapadia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Lie Generator Networks (LGN)，通过矩阵指数直接计算轨迹，而非积分，从而保持物理结构。通过参数化A = S - D（斜对称减正对角），稳定性和耗散性从架构中自然产生，无需通过损失函数引入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性动力系统有精确的闭式解，但现有神经方法在从数据推断系统参数时，要么缺乏物理保证（如Neural ODEs可能违反物理不变量），要么无法表示真实系统中的耗散（如能量守恒架构）。需要一种既能保持物理结构又能表示耗散的方法。

Method: 提出Lie Generator Networks (LGN)，学习结构化生成器A，通过矩阵指数直接计算轨迹。参数化A = S - D，其中S是斜对称矩阵，D是正对角矩阵。这种架构从构造上保证了稳定性和耗散性。

Result: 在100维稳定RLC梯形网络上，标准最小二乘系统辨识可能产生不稳定特征值，无约束LGN产生稳定但不正确的频谱，而LGN-SD恢复所有100个特征值，特征值误差比无约束替代方法低两个数量级。这些特征值揭示了可解释的物理量（极点、固有频率、阻尼比）。

Conclusion: LGN为线性保守、耗散和时变系统提供了统一框架，通过架构而非损失函数保证物理结构，提供可解释的物理量，优于黑盒神经网络方法。

Abstract: When the system is linear, why should learning be nonlinear? Linear dynamical systems, the analytical backbone of control theory, signal processing and circuit analysis, have exact closed-form solutions via the state transition matrix. Yet when system parameters must be inferred from data, recent neural approaches offer flexibility at the cost of physical guarantees: Neural ODEs provide flexible trajectory approximation but may violate physical invariants, while energy preserving architectures do not natively represent dissipation essential to real-world systems. We introduce Lie Generator Networks (LGN), which learn a structured generator A and compute trajectories directly via matrix exponentiation. This shift from integration to exponentiation preserves structure by construction. By parameterizing A = S - D (skew-symmetric minus positive diagonal), stability and dissipation emerge from the underlying architecture and are not introduced during training via the loss function. LGN provides a unified framework for linear conservative, dissipative, and time-varying systems. On a 100-dimensional stable RLC ladder, standard derivative-based least-squares system identification can yield unstable eigenvalues. The unconstrained LGN yields stable but physically incorrect spectra, whereas LGN-SD recovers all 100 eigenvalues with over two orders of magnitude lower mean eigenvalue error than unconstrained alternatives. Critically, these eigenvalues reveal poles, natural frequencies, and damping ratios which are interpretable physics that black-box networks do not provide.

</details>


### [34] [Distributed Online Submodular Maximization under Communication Delays: A Simultaneous Decision-Making Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27803)
*Zirui Xu,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分布式在线贪婪算法解决多智能体次模最大化问题，在任意网络拓扑下处理通信延迟，平衡协调性能与收敛时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来分布式信息收集任务需要在未知动态环境中进行，效用函数具有递减回报特性（次模性）。现有方法要么依赖顺序多跳通信导致延迟过高，要么限制智能体只能与一跳邻居协调，限制了协调性能。

Method: 提出分布式在线贪婪算法，结合对抗性多臂赌博机学习与延迟反馈技术，支持任意网络拓扑下的同时决策，分析网络结构对次优性的影响。

Result: 算法提供了相对于最优解的近似性能保证，揭示了协调性能与收敛时间之间的权衡关系，该权衡由通信延迟大小决定。

Conclusion: 该算法填补了完全集中式在线协调方法与完全分布式一跳协调方法之间的空白，通过通信延迟的权衡实现了性能与收敛时间的平衡。

Abstract: We provide a distributed online algorithm for multi-agent submodular maximization under communication delays. We are motivated by the future distributed information-gathering tasks in unknown and dynamic environments, where utility functions naturally exhibit the diminishing-returns property, i.e., submodularity. Existing approaches for online submodular maximization either rely on sequential multi-hop communication, resulting in prohibitive delays and restrictive connectivity assumptions, or restrict each agent's coordination to its one-hop neighborhood only, thereby limiting the coordination performance. To address the issue, we provide the Distributed Online Greedy (DOG) algorithm, which integrates tools from adversarial bandit learning with delayed feedback to enable simultaneous decision-making across arbitrary network topologies. We provide the approximation performance of DOG against an optimal solution, capturing the suboptimality cost due to decentralization as a function of the network structure. Our analyses further reveal a trade-off between coordination performance and convergence time, determined by the magnitude of communication delays. By this trade-off, DOG spans the spectrum between the state-of-the-art fully centralized online coordination approach [1] and fully decentralized one-hop coordination approach [2].

</details>


### [35] [Mitigating Forgetting in Continual Learning with Selective Gradient Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26671)
*Anika Singh,Aayush Dhaulakhandi,Varun Chopade,Likhith Malipati,David Martinez,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SFAO通过余弦相似度和逐层门控机制动态调节梯度方向，在持续学习中实现选择性遗忘，在保持竞争力的同时大幅降低90%内存成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在动态环境中部署时面临灾难性遗忘问题，即学习新任务时会覆盖先前学到的知识，导致早期任务性能严重下降。现有方法在平衡可塑性和稳定性方面存在挑战。

Method: 提出选择性遗忘感知优化（SFAO），通过余弦相似度调节梯度方向，采用逐层门控机制，使用可调机制选择性地投影、接受或丢弃更新，并通过高效的蒙特卡洛近似实现。

Result: 在标准持续学习基准测试中，SFAO实现了竞争力的准确率，同时内存成本显著降低90%，在MNIST数据集上表现出改进的遗忘控制。

Conclusion: SFAO通过选择性遗忘机制有效平衡了可塑性和稳定性，在保持性能的同时大幅降低资源消耗，适用于资源受限的场景。

Abstract: As neural networks are increasingly deployed in dynamic environments, they face the challenge of catastrophic forgetting, the tendency to overwrite previously learned knowledge when adapting to new tasks, resulting in severe performance degradation on earlier tasks. We propose Selective Forgetting-Aware Optimization (SFAO), a dynamic method that regulates gradient directions via cosine similarity and per-layer gating, enabling controlled forgetting while balancing plasticity and stability. SFAO selectively projects, accepts, or discards updates using a tunable mechanism with efficient Monte Carlo approximation. Experiments on standard continual learning benchmarks show that SFAO achieves competitive accuracy with markedly lower memory cost, a 90$\%$ reduction, and improved forgetting on MNIST datasets, making it suitable for resource-constrained scenarios.

</details>


### [36] [Boundary-aware Prototype-driven Adversarial Alignment for Cross-Corpus EEG Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26713)
*Guangli Li,Canbiao Wu,Na Tian,Li Zhang,Zhen Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出原型驱动的对抗对齐框架，通过原型引导的局部类别对齐、对比语义正则化和边界感知聚合，提升跨数据集EEG情绪识别的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的情绪识别在跨异构数据集时性能严重下降，现有域对抗方法主要关注全局边缘对齐，忽略了类别条件不匹配和决策边界扭曲问题，限制了跨语料库的泛化能力。

Method: 提出统一的原型驱动对抗对齐框架，包含三个渐进配置：PAA-L进行原型引导的局部类别条件对齐；PAA-C加入对比语义正则化增强类内紧凑性和类间分离性；PAA-M整合双关系感知分类器，采用三阶段对抗优化方案显式细化决策边界附近的争议样本。

Result: 在SEED、SEED-IV和SEED-V数据集上，四种跨语料库评估协议下均取得最先进性能，平均提升分别为6.72%、5.59%、6.69%和4.83%。框架在临床抑郁症识别场景中也有效泛化，验证了其在真实异构环境中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将原型引导的子域对齐、对比判别增强和边界感知聚合结合在统一的对抗架构中，该框架将情绪识别重新定义为关系驱动的表示学习问题，降低了对标签噪声的敏感性并提高了跨域稳定性。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based emotion recognition suffers from severe performance degradation when models are transferred across heterogeneous datasets due to physiological variability, experimental paradigm differences, and device inconsistencies. Existing domain adversarial methods primarily enforce global marginal alignment and often overlook class-conditional mismatch and decision boundary distortion, limiting cross-corpus generalization. In this work, we propose a unified Prototype-driven Adversarial Alignment (PAA) framework for cross-corpus EEG emotion recognition. The framework is progressively instantiated in three configurations: PAA-L, which performs prototype-guided local class-conditional alignment; PAA-C, which further incorporates contrastive semantic regularization to enhance intra-class compactness and inter-class separability; and PAA-M, the full boundary-aware configuration that integrates dual relation-aware classifiers within a three-stage adversarial optimization scheme to explicitly refine controversial samples near decision boundaries. By combining prototype-guided subdomain alignment, contrastive discriminative enhancement, and boundary-aware aggregation within a coherent adversarial architecture, the proposed framework reformulates emotion recognition as a relation-driven representation learning problem, reducing sensitivity to label noise and improving cross-domain stability. Extensive experiments on SEED, SEED-IV, and SEED-V demonstrate state-of-the-art performance under four cross-corpus evaluation protocols, with average improvements of 6.72\%, 5.59\%, 6.69\%, and 4.83\%, respectively. Furthermore, the proposed framework generalizes effectively to clinical depression identification scenarios, validating its robustness in real-world heterogeneous settings. The source code is available at \textit{https://github.com/WuCB-BCI/PAA}

</details>


### [37] [Learning to Select Visual In-Context Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26775)
*Eugene Lee,Yu-Chi Lin,Jiajie Diao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LSD（Learning to Select Demonstrations）方法，使用强化学习训练智能体为多模态大语言模型选择最优演示示例，相比传统的kNN方法在客观事实回归任务上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型通过上下文学习适应视觉任务，但演示示例的质量对性能影响很大。主流的kNN选择策略基于相似性优先，在复杂的客观事实回归任务中存在局限性，会选择冗余示例而无法覆盖任务的完整输出范围。

Method: 将演示选择重新定义为序列决策问题，引入LSD方法训练强化学习智能体构建最优演示集。使用Dueling DQN架构和查询中心Transformer解码器，学习最大化MLLM下游性能的策略。

Result: 在五个视觉回归基准测试中，发现关键二分现象：kNN在主观偏好任务中仍保持最优，而LSD在客观事实回归任务上显著优于基线方法。通过平衡视觉相关性和多样性，LSD能更好地定义回归边界。

Conclusion: LSD方法揭示了何时需要学习型选择策略来优化视觉上下文学习，特别是在客观事实回归任务中，学习型选择是必要的，而主观偏好任务仍适合简单的kNN方法。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) adapt to visual tasks via in-context learning (ICL), which relies heavily on demonstration quality. The dominant demonstration selection strategy is unsupervised k-Nearest Neighbor (kNN) search. While simple, this similarity-first approach is sub-optimal for complex factual regression tasks; it selects redundant examples that fail to capture the task's full output range. We reframe selection as a sequential decision-making problem and introduce Learning to Select Demonstrations (LSD), training a Reinforcement Learning agent to construct optimal demonstration sets. Using a Dueling DQN with a query-centric Transformer Decoder, our agent learns a policy that maximizes MLLM downstream performance. Evaluating across five visual regression benchmarks, we uncover a crucial dichotomy: while kNN remains optimal for subjective preference tasks, LSD significantly outperforms baselines on objective, factual regression tasks. By balancing visual relevance with diversity, LSD better defines regression boundaries, illuminating when learned selection is strictly necessary for visual ICL.

</details>


### [38] [TED: Training-Free Experience Distillation for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26778)
*Shuozhi Yuan,Jinqing Wang,Zihao Liu,Miaomiao Yuan,Haoran Peng,Jin Zhao,Bingwen Wang,Haoyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TED提出了一种无需训练、基于上下文的蒸馏框架，将知识蒸馏从模型参数更新转移到提示中的上下文经验注入，通过经验压缩机制解决上下文增长和噪声积累问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏需要重复参数更新和大规模训练数据，在资源受限环境中应用受限。需要一种无需训练参数更新、低数据依赖的蒸馏方法。

Method: TED框架：1) 学生生成多个推理轨迹，教师独立生成解决方案；2) 教师比较学生轨迹与自身推理及真实答案，提取通用经验；3) 经验持续精炼更新；4) 引入经验压缩机制，跟踪使用统计，选择性合并、重写或删除低效用经验。

Result: 在MathVision基准上，TED将Qwen3-VL-8B性能从0.627提升到0.702；在VisualPuzzles上，仅用100个训练样本从0.517提升到0.561。在低数据、无更新设置下，性能与完全训练的基于参数的蒸馏竞争，同时减少5倍以上训练成本。

Conclusion: TED证明了通过上下文经验可以实现有意义的知识转移，为资源受限环境下的知识蒸馏提供了有效的训练免费替代方案。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is typically realized by transferring a teacher model's knowledge into a student's parameters through supervised or reinforcement-based optimization. While effective, such approaches require repeated parameter updates and large-scale training data, limiting their applicability in resource-constrained environments. In this work, we propose TED, a training-free, context-based distillation framework that shifts the update target of distillation from model parameters to an in-context experience injected into the student's prompt. For each input, the student generates multiple reasoning trajectories, while a teacher independently produces its own solution. The teacher then compares the student trajectories with its reasoning and the ground-truth answer, extracting generalized experiences that capture effective reasoning patterns. These experiences are continuously refined and updated over time. A key challenge of context-based distillation is unbounded experience growth and noise accumulation. TED addresses this with an experience compression mechanism that tracks usage statistics and selectively merges, rewrites, or removes low-utility experiences. Experiments on multimodal reasoning benchmarks MathVision and VisualPuzzles show that TED consistently improves performance. On MathVision, TED raises the performance of Qwen3-VL-8B from 0.627 to 0.702, and on VisualPuzzles from 0.517 to 0.561 with just 100 training samples. Under this low-data, no-update setting, TED achieves performance competitive with fully trained parameter-based distillation while reducing training cost by over 5x, demonstrating that meaningful knowledge transfer can be achieved through contextual experience.

</details>


### [39] [A Step Toward Federated Pretraining of Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26786)
*Baochen Xiong,Yifan Xu,Xiaoshan Yang,Yaguang Song,Yaowei Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fed-CMP：首个联邦多模态大语言模型预训练框架，通过规范可靠性感知聚合和正交保持动量解决参数干扰和梯度振荡问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型发展受限于高质量公开数据饱和，而大量多模态数据分散在隐私敏感的数据孤岛中。联邦学习虽能利用分布式资源，但现有研究主要关注微调阶段，基础预训练阶段尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出Fed-CMP框架：1）规范可靠性感知聚合：构建规范空间将客户端投影器分解为共享对齐基和客户端特定系数，进行可靠性加权融合抑制参数干扰；2）正交保持动量：通过正交投影对共享对齐基应用动量，积累历史优化方向同时保持几何结构。

Result: 基于公开数据集构建四个联邦预训练场景，大量实验验证Fed-CMP显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: Fed-CMP是首个针对联邦多模态大语言模型预训练的框架，通过解决参数干扰和梯度振荡问题，有效利用分布式隐私敏感数据进行模型预训练。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) is bottlenecked by the saturation of high-quality public data, while vast amounts of diverse multimodal data remain inaccessible in privacy-sensitive silos. Federated Learning (FL) offers a promising solution to unlock these distributed resources, but existing research focuses predominantly on fine-tuning, leaving the foundational pre-training phase largely unexplored. In this paper, we formally introduce the Federated MLLM Alignment (Fed-MA) task, a lightweight pre-training paradigm that freezes the vision encoder and LLM while collaboratively training the cross-modal projector. We identify two critical challenges in this setting: (i) parameter interference in aggregating local projectors; and (ii) gradient oscillations in one-pass collaborative SGD. To address these challenges, we propose Fed-CMP, a pioneering framework for federated MLLM pre-training. Fed-CMP employs Canonical Reliability-Aware Aggregation, which constructs a canonical space to decompose client projectors into a shared alignment basis and client-specific coefficients, then performs reliability-weighted fusion to suppress parameter interference. Furthermore, Fed-CMP introduces Orthogonality-Preserved Momentum, which applies momentum to the shared alignment basis via orthogonal projection, accumulating historical optimization directions while preserving geometric structure. We construct four federated pre-training scenarios based on public datasets, and extensive experiments validate that Fed-CMP significantly outperforms existing baselines.

</details>


### [40] [Robust Batch-Level Query Routing for Large Language Models under Cost and Capacity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26796)
*Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Kayhan Behdin,Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Zhipeng Wang,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个批处理级别的资源感知路由框架，用于在成本、GPU资源和并发约束下优化LLM查询路由，相比逐查询方法提升显著


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逐查询路由方法难以控制批处理级别的成本，特别是在非均匀或对抗性批处理场景下，需要更有效的资源约束路由方案

Method: 提出批处理级别的资源感知路由框架，联合优化每个批次的模型分配；引入鲁棒变体处理LLM性能预测不确定性；设计离线实例分配程序平衡多模型的质量和吞吐量

Result: 鲁棒性提升准确率1-14%；批处理路由在对抗性批处理下比逐查询方法提升24%；优化实例分配带来额外3%增益；同时严格控制成本和GPU资源约束

Conclusion: 批处理级别的资源感知路由框架能有效解决LLM查询路由中的资源约束问题，在保持成本控制的同时显著提升性能

Abstract: We study the problem of routing queries to large language models (LLMs) under cost, GPU resources, and concurrency constraints. Prior per-query routing methods often fail to control batch-level cost, especially under non-uniform or adversarial batching. To address this, we propose a batch-level, resource-aware routing framework that jointly optimizes model assignment for each batch while respecting cost and model capacity limits. We further introduce a robust variant that accounts for uncertainty in predicted LLM performance, along with an offline instance allocation procedure that balances quality and throughput across multiple models. Experiments on two multi-task LLM benchmarks show that robustness improves accuracy by 1-14% over non-robust counterparts (depending on the performance estimator), batch-level routing outperforms per-query methods by up to 24% under adversarial batching, and optimized instance allocation yields additional gains of up to 3% compared to a non-optimized allocation, all while strictly controlling cost and GPU resource constraints.

</details>


### [41] [MemGuard-Alpha: Detecting and Filtering Memorization-Contaminated Signals in LLM-Based Financial Forecasting via Membership Inference and Cross-Model Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26797)
*Anisha Roy,Dip Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MemGuard-Alpha：一个后生成框架，通过组合五种成员推理攻击方法和跨模型记忆分歧，有效过滤LLM生成的金融信号中的记忆污染，显著提升样本外表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成金融alpha信号时存在记忆历史数据的问题，导致虚假的预测准确性，在样本外表现崩溃。现有解决方案（模型重训练和输入匿名化）要么成本过高，要么造成信息损失，缺乏实用的实时交易信号级过滤方法。

Method: 提出MemGuard-Alpha框架，包含两个算法：1) MemGuard Composite Score (MCS)：结合五种成员推理攻击方法和时间邻近特征，通过逻辑回归生成综合评分；2) Cross-Model Memorization Disagreement (CMMD)：利用不同LLM训练截止日期的差异，分离记忆信号与真实推理信号。

Result: 在7个LLM、50只S&P 100股票、42,800个提示和5.5年数据上的评估显示：CMMD实现夏普比率4.11（未过滤信号为2.76，提升49%）；清洁信号平均日回报14.48 bps，污染信号仅2.13 bps（7倍差异）；发现交叉模式：样本内准确性随污染增加而上升（40.8%到52.5%），样本外准确性则下降（47%到42%）。

Conclusion: MemGuard-Alpha提供了一种实用、零成本的信号级过滤方法，能有效识别和过滤LLM生成的金融信号中的记忆污染，显著改善样本外表现，为LLM在量化金融中的可靠应用提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate financial alpha signals, yet growing evidence shows that LLMs memorize historical financial data from their training corpora, producing spurious predictive accuracy that collapses out-of-sample. This memorization-induced look-ahead bias threatens the validity of LLM-based quantitative strategies. Prior remedies -- model retraining and input anonymization -- are either prohibitively expensive or introduce significant information loss. No existing method offers practical, zero-cost signal-level filtering for real-time trading. We introduce MemGuard-Alpha, a post-generation framework comprising two algorithms: (i) the MemGuard Composite Score (MCS), which combines five membership inference attack (MIA) methods with temporal proximity features via logistic regression, achieving Cohen's d = 18.57 for contamination separation (d = 0.39-1.37 using MIA features alone); and (ii) Cross-Model Memorization Disagreement (CMMD), which exploits variation in training cutoff dates across LLMs to separate memorized signals from genuine reasoning. Evaluated across seven LLMs (124M-7B parameters), 50 S&P 100 stocks, 42,800 prompts, and five MIA methods over 5.5 years (2019-2024), CMMD achieves a Sharpe ratio of 4.11 versus 2.76 for unfiltered signals (49% improvement). Clean signals produce 14.48 bps average daily return versus 2.13 bps for tainted signals (7x difference). A striking crossover pattern emerges: in-sample accuracy rises with contamination (40.8% to 52.5%) while out-of-sample accuracy falls (47% to 42%), providing direct evidence that memorization inflates apparent accuracy at the cost of generalization.

</details>


### [42] [A Comparative Investigation of Thermodynamic Structure-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26803)
*Guojie Li,Liu Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PINNs中不同热力学结构嵌入方式的系统比较：牛顿力学、拉格朗日力学、哈密顿力学用于保守系统，Onsager变分原理和扩展不可逆热力学用于耗散系统，评估其对精度、物理一致性、噪声鲁棒性和可解释性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs的性能和物理一致性强烈依赖于如何融入控制定律，需要系统比较不同热力学结构嵌入方式的效果，为热力学一致性模型提供设计指导。

Method: 通过系统比较不同热力学结构信息神经网络：牛顿力学、拉格朗日力学、哈密顿力学用于保守系统；Onsager变分原理和扩展不可逆热力学用于耗散系统。在代表性常微分方程和偏微分方程上进行综合数值实验。

Result: 基于牛顿残差的PINNs可以重建系统状态，但无法可靠恢复关键物理和热力学量；而结构保持公式显著增强了参数识别、热力学一致性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 为热力学一致性模型的原则性设计提供实用指导，并为将更一般的非平衡热力学结构集成到物理信息机器学习中奠定基础。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) offer a unified framework for solving both forward and inverse problems of differential equations, yet their performance and physical consistency strongly depend on how governing laws are incorporated. In this work, we present a systematic comparison of different thermodynamic structure-informed neural networks by incorporating various thermodynamics formulations, including Newtonian, Lagrangian, and Hamiltonian mechanics for conservative systems, as well as the Onsager variational principle and extended irreversible thermodynamics for dissipative systems.
